====================================================================================================
LADDER AS EXECUTED
====================================================================================================
  'GA accesses in window' counts every access between this dispatch and the next - which
  INCLUDES interrupt traffic, since the record ISRs run while the ladder sits on an op.
  op42's huge count is the whole read: the ladder parks there while the records arrive.

    6.41399  micro-op $24 op24  settle/spin-up arm                                       dispatches=1      GA accesses in window=5
    6.41407  micro-op $28 op28  seek + wait for spin-up ([$7a36]); sets [$798e]          dispatches=14633  GA accesses in window=121
    7.95856  micro-op $56 op56  parse; sets [$796a] from UIB+$11 bit0 + cmd              dispatches=1      GA accesses in window=0
    7.95957  micro-op $58 op58  clamp the transfer count -> [$7abc]/[$7abe]              dispatches=1      GA accesses in window=0
    7.95970  micro-op $18 op18  arm the field program (C800 bit-positions + E000 program dispatches=1      GA accesses in window=64
    7.95994  micro-op $54 op54  copy the watch template ($66A/$6A6) + patch rec2's watch dispatches=1      GA accesses in window=32
    7.96018  micro-op $4A op4A  drain request: arm [$7b10], fork on [$7968]              dispatches=1      GA accesses in window=0
    7.96038  micro-op $42 op42  guard/wait; UIB+$12 bit7 bypasses the timer              dispatches=927    GA accesses in window=11053
